- **data-minimization-designer**: Design data-minimized pipelines that collect only necessary fields, enforce purpose limitation, set storage limits, and apply privacy-by-design principles from the earliest stage. ([HossamBalaha/SkillStack](https://github.com/HossamBalaha/SkillStack/tree/HEAD/ethics-compliance/data-minimization-designer))
- **data-minimization-desk**: make field-level necessity determinations tied to a named purpose, choose pseudonymization, tokenization, aggregation, or generalization with the key custody that keeps the technique meaningful, run re-identification assessments naming the auxiliary data considered, and hold the boundary between pseudonymized data that stays in scope and anonymous data that leaves it. use for data minimization reviews, field necessity audits, de-identification and anonymization design, k-anonymity and differential privacy choices, tokenization and key custody, analytics and model training datasets, and over-c… ([MadewellRD/skills-lab](https://github.com/MadewellRD/skills-lab/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/privacy-data-protection-command-desk/data-minimization-desk) · ★2 · 4 copies)

- **data-minimization-review**: Reduce personal-data collection and persistence to what is necessary for approved product purposes while preserving required functionality and evidence. ([Dadmin88/hermes-profile-packs](https://github.com/Dadmin88/hermes-profile-packs/tree/HEAD/hermes-agency/profiles/agency-privacy-engineer/skills/data-minimization-review) · ★49)
- **data-minimization-review**: Use when reviewing an existing system, feature, or dataset for excess personal data. Covers field level collection audits, retention review, log and analytics scrubbing, and turning findings into drop, mask, or shorten decisions. Use when a product ships new fields, when a privacy review flags overcollection, or when preparing evidence that only necessary data is kept. - **data-mining**: Data Mining. Unsupervised pattern discovery - clustering and customer segmentation (k-means, k-medoids, hierarchical, DBSCAN, HDBSCAN, Gaussian mixtures), association rules and market basket analysis (Apriori, FP-Growth, support, confidence, lift), anomaly and outlier detection (Isolation Forest, LOF, one-class SVM, robust z-scores, autoencoders), dimensionality reduction for exploration (PCA, t-SNE, UMAP), sequence mining, recommenders and text mining. Use for segmentation, clustering, unsupervised learning, basket analysis, fraud or anomaly screening, topic discovery. ([Harrypatria/data-mining-ml](https://github.com/Harrypatria/data-mining-ml/tree/HEAD/skills/data-mining) · ★2)
- **data-mining**: Extract patterns and insights from large datasets using association rules, clustering, classification, and anomaly detection. Use for market basket analysis, customer segmentation, fraud detection, pattern discovery, predictive modeling, and knowledge extraction from structured and unstructured data. ([makaronz/skilslsls](https://github.com/makaronz/skilslsls/tree/HEAD/data-mining) · 2 copies)
